Document 103: 261027_daiwa_faraday

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_daiwa_faraday.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Faraday released a mixed set of results and guidance on 27 
October. 3Q26 core results missed consensus on an unfavourable product 
mix and higher opex, while 4Q26 guidance was broadly intact on delayed 
MCU business rebalancing. Regardless of the near-term hiccup, 
management expects its 3 product lines of mass-production (MP), IP and 
non-recurrent engineering (NRE) to fire on all cylinders into 2027 for a 
double-digit % YoY revenue growth. This concurs with our turnaround story 
for the counter, and we reiterate our Buy (1) rating and 12M TP of TWD220.

What’s the impact: Results mixed, guidance intact. Faraday’s 3Q26 net 
profit came in at TWD279m, 9% above our estimate and beating 
consensus by a meaningful 38%. Despite the beat, dynamics were mixed 
(table on p.2). Core operations missed despite a higher top line, which was 
spurred by MP on stronger-than-expected MCU sales from its 50%-owned 
Artery (6907 TT, not rated) for a lower gross margin (GM) due to an 
unfavourable revenue mix as MP carries a lower GM than the other 2 lines. 
Higher opex, as a result of salary hikes and employee bonuses, further 
pressured core operations with operating profit missing our/consensus 
estimates by 36%/11%, but non-operating gains from marked-to-market 
investments more than offset the miss. Guidance calls for a roughly flat 
4Q26 top line with a 45% GM, broadly in line with our expectation.

Firing on all cylinders across all 3 lines. In our previous note, we 
expected rebalancing in the MCU business due to demand pull-in on price 
hikes and supply chain uncertainties. It failed to happen in 3Q26 thanks to 
early pull-in of OSAT capacity support, thus pushing back the rebalancing 
to 4Q26 — a key reason why top line was guided to be flat despite the 
other 2 lines of IP and NRE growing QoQ. That said, management expects 
the MCU business to return to a growth track in 2027 after the near-term 
hiccup. Its delayed advanced packaging (AP) ramp on design complexity 
and supply constraint looks set to accelerate in 2027 on OSAT capacity 
improvement which, together with a milestone of 2nm/GAA design-win for a 
cloud ASIC, should add to upside for the NRE and MP business next year. 
All in, we lower our 2026-27E EPS by 9-13% to factor in the higher opex.

What we recommend: Despite our forecast revisions, we keep our 12M 
TP for Faraday unchanged at TWD220 as we maintain our 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 37x. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating with key 
downside risks of silicon inflation and geopolitical overhangs leading to 
global inflation and prolonging a non-AI recovery.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 6-9% below the consensus 
forecasts likely on the street’s lower opex assumptions.

27 October 2026

Turnaround confirmed (II)

➢ 3Q26 results a mixed bag; 4Q26 guidance broadly intact 
➢ Firing on all cylinders into 2027 after near-term hiccups 
➢ Reiterating our Buy (1) call and 12M TP of TWD220

Company profile 
Founded in 1993, Faraday is a hybrid design service provider offering IP and ASIC design services to 
fabless, foundry and system houses. It has R&D and marketing locations in the US, Japan, China, India, 
and Vietnam.

### Page 5

5

Faraday Technology (3035 TT): 27 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 10 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Faraday’s Board of Directors (BoD) consists of 10 members, including 4 independent directors,
representing 40% of the board—significantly above the regulatory minimum of 20%. Three of the
10 directors are female, reflecting progress toward gender diversity at the board level. The roles
of Chairman and President are separated, supporting stronger governance checks and
balances. However, most non independent directors concurrently serve as executive managers,
which may elevate concerns regarding board independence and management oversight from an
ESG governance perspective.
Capital management 1
Over the past 10 years, Faraday has kept its dividend payout ratio at above 70% most of the
time, in spite of an up or down cycle. As an IC design service company without capex for
manufacturing fabs, Faraday's high dividend payout is appropriate, in our view.
Related party &
transaction 1
UMC, which is Faraday's largest shareholder, is Faraday's major foundry for its turnkey
business. For IC design service companies, a close relationship with foundries is essential since
their design should build on a foundry's process node and their turnkey businesses also require
a foundry's capacity support. UMC's support offers Faraday a competitive advantage, in our
view. Faraday has developed many standard cell and I/O libraries for UMC, covering logic and
mixed-signal specialty processes across 0.5μ to 22nm.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
Faraday has set several evaluation standards for its existing and new suppliers and it requires all
suppliers to get ISO9001 and ISO14001 certifications for quality and environmental
management. In 2024, 100% of its wafer, packaging and testing providers had ISO9001 and
ISO14001 certifications. In addition, Faraday also has on-site audits (quality system and process
quality audit, CSR evaluation and environment management system) for its suppliers with a mid-
to-long term target of conducting annual onsite audits for 100% of its suppliers.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 1
As an IC design service company, Faraday outsources its manufacturing and thus has no own
production sites. As such, Faraday's water use is primarily for general operations, not for
production. Faraday continues to conduct several water reduction measures to manage water
usage.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
Faraday has established a green supply chain in accordance with the EU Directive on the
Restriction of the Use of Certain Hazardous Substances in Electrical and Electronic Equipment
(RoHS) and with the EU Chemicals Policy (REACH).
Faraday has a mid-to-long term target of carefully evaluating and selecting raw materials with
low hazard levels to comply with the Hazardous Substance-Free Product Standard. All of its
packaging materials are 100% lead-free and halogen-free.
E GHG emissions 1
With Faraday's energy saving measures, it reduced the GHG emissions intensity gradually. As
an IC design service company without onsite production, Faraday's GHG emissions are much
lower than other semiconductor manufacturing companies. Faraday's low power design also
helps reduce GHG emissions. In 2019, Faraday completed the development of its ultra-low
power consumption 22nm IP solution.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

Hon Hai Precision Industry

HHTD 2026: precision manufacturing, 
vertical integration, AI DC, and new EV

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 320.00 TWD | Price: 245.50 TWD

7th HHTD: precision manufacturing, vertical integration 
Hon Hai hosted its seventh Tech Day, titled "Accelerating Intelligence" on 30 Oct. Key 
highlights included its strengths in precision manufacturing, vertical integration, robotics 
and AI datacenters, as well as the launch of the Model A+ and Model K/K+ EVs. We 
expect Hon Hai, with its strong manufacturing capability and vertical integration 
advantages, to sustain its leading position as an AI server ODM. Maintain Buy. 
Doubled AI rack production to 2k racks per week currently  
Hon Hai's Chairman indicated that the company has shortened the assembly time for a 
compute tray from 2 hours to 20 minutes. This was supported by its precision 
manufacturing capability (1µm precision), automation and a cableless design co-
developed with its customer. Amid continued strong AI demand, Hon Hai has doubled its 
AI server rack production from 1,000 racks per week a year ago to 2,000 racks per week. 
AI server solution offerings across L1-L12 and CPO 
The company can now provide up to 50% of server rack components (excluding 
GPUs/CPUs), with solution offerings spanning L1 to L12. It demonstrated its high-speed 
cables/connectors, power busbars/whips, liquid cooling solutions (manifolds/cold 
plates/UQDs), compute/switch trays including the NVIDIA Spectrum-X CPO switch tray, 
server racks, as well as SuperPOD and AI datacenter solutions. 
Robotics for factory automation and new EV 
Hon Hai showcased live demonstrations of its robotics, highlighting capabilities such as 
AI-planned assembly paths and real-time sensing and tracking. For EVs, the company 
launched Model A+ and Model K/K+. Model A+ features L4 readiness, four-wheel drive 
and a spacious cabin. Model K converts from a truck into a van with a canopy box, while 
Model K+ features a more stylish design to serve the Japanese and European markets.

Company Sector 
Electronics Manufacturing Services

Company Description 
Hon Hai is the world's largest EMS provider, with four key 
business segments - Smart Consumer Electronics, Cloud & 
Networking, Computing, and Components & Others. Hon 
Hai supplies components, final assembly, and global 
logistics for global tech giants, including Apple, Microsoft, 
Google, HP, Dell, Cisco, and Sony. Hon Hai has 
R&D/production sites globally, including Taiwan, China, 
India, Japan, Vietnam, Malaysia, Czech Republic, Mexico, and 
the US. The company was established in Taiwan in 1974.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Hon Hai as its gradual moves into 
high margin/value-added server/components/semi 
business, stronger position in AI server with rising sales, 
solid balance sheet, and attractive valuation among Greater 
China AI server players.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Hon Hai Precision Industry (HNHAF) 
We set our PO at NT$320 (15x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS), at around 3SD above historical P/E 
average since 2007. We think the valuation is justified as HH's leading position in 
EMS/ODM business, improving iPhone momentum with replacement cycle, rising sales 
from AI business, more diversified global footprint than peers to mitigate 
tariff/geopolitical risk, and improving balance sheet with higher cash dividend payout.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker momentum from Apple business (iPhone, iPad 
and Apple Watch), (2) increasing competition in EMS business from peers, (3) slower 
sales/earnings delivery in auto/EV business, and (4) weaker market demand or lower 
shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better-than-expected yield rate and demand for iPhone, 
iPad and Apple Watch business, (2) further automation adoption to reduce labor costs 
and improve efficiency, (3) faster sales/earnings delivery from auto/EV business, and (4) 
stronger market demand or higher shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(L TA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 L TA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 L TA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 L TA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 29 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성됐음을 확인합니다. 
본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를 
기관투자자 또는 제3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (박준영, 김나우) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 5

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

3Q26 전사 예상 영업이익 100조원 
DS부문 예상 영업이익은 100.0조원(OPM 67.4%, YoY +1538.5%, QoQ +14.5%) 으로, 
달러환율 1,380원기준, 메모리와 비메모리 사업부문 예상 영업손익은 각각 102.0조원,    
-2.0조원으로 추정한다. 1) 메모리반도체 가격 상승세가 이어지는 가운데, HBM4 판매 
본격화에 따른 영향을 고려하면 DRAM/NAND 분기 평균 가격 상승폭은 각각 +18.0%, 
+16.0%으로 추정하고, 2)비메모리반도체 사업 적자는 지속될 전망이기 때문이다. 다만 
특별 성과급 지급에 따른 충당금 영향을 제외하고 선단공정 중심의 파운드리 가동률을 
고려하면 적자폭은 유의미하게 감소한 것으로 파악된다는 점이 긍정적이다.   
SDC부문 예상 영업이익은 1.1조원(OPM 13.0%, YoY -9.6%, QoQ +54.9%)으로, 1)국내 
주력 고객사향 폴더블 스마트폰 판매 호조가 사업부의 외형 성장에 긍정적으로 작용했을 
것으로 추정됨에도 불구하고, 2) 8.6세대 IT OLED 패널 양산 개시에 따른 감가상각비(약 
0.3조원 수준)와 북미 고객 중심의 판가 인하 압박 영향으로 전년동기대비 수익성은 약 
2%pt 훼손이 불가피할 전망이다. DX부문 예상 영업손익은 -1.1조원(YoY 적자전환, QoQ 
적자지속)으로, 메모리반도체를 포함한 IT 부품 가격 부담이 전분기대비 확대되고 있기 
때문이다. MX와 VD/DA사업부 예상 손익은 각각 -0.9조원, -0.2조원으로 추정한다.

투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 63만원으로 상향 조정  
메모리반도체의 가격 상승폭이 다소 둔화되겠지만, 2027~2028년에도 타이트한 수급 
환경이 이어질 전망이라는 점을 주목한다. 1)시장은 신규 증설과 HBM 관련 노이즈로 인한 
2028년 피크아웃 가능성을 우려하고 있으나, HBM 의 범용 DRAM 생산능력 잠식과 AI 
추론 확산에 따른 고용량 메모리 수요를 감안하면 공급 제약의 해소 시점은 예상보다 
늦어질 가능성이 높기 때문이다. 뿐만 아니라, 2)메모리 업사이클이 장기화될 경우 동사 
FCF는 과거대비 구조적으로 확대되어 보다 친화적인 주주환원 정책(2027~2029년, 
2027년 1월 예정)을 제시할 가능성도 배제할 수 없기 때문이다. 동사 2027년 연간 실적 
추정치 상향 조정에 따라 목표주가를 63만원(2027년 BPS * Target PBR 3.5배 적용)으로 
상향 조정한다. 피크아웃 우려에 따른 주가 조정은 중장기 관점에서 매수 기회다.

 절대 (24.9) (6.2) (23.5)  
 상대 (13.1) (10.5) (47.4)  
 절대 (달러환산) (23.8) 1.5  (18.0)

3Q26 Review: “조삼모사” Again  
3Q26P 연결 실적은 매출액 6조 943억원(YoY -6.0%), 영업이익 4,520억원(YoY -46.2%, OPM 
7.4%)을 기록하며 컨센서스 대비 연결 매출액(7.5조원)과 영업이익(1.16조원) 모두 대폭 하회했다. 주
요 자회사가 양호한 실적을 기록했음에도 불구하고, 지상방산 실적이 매우 부진했다. 지상방산은 매출
액 1조 2,455억원(YoY -41.0%), 영업이익 217억원(YoY -96.2%, OPM 1.7%)을 기록했는데, 1)3Q 
생산 물량이 4Q 매출 인식되는 흐름 속, 고정비 부담이 컸었고, 2)낮은 수출 비중(3Q 48%, 2Q 58%)
과 국내외 사업의 부진한 믹스(국내 BEP 수준, 해외 OPM 11% 추정), 3)비우호적인 원/달러 환율, 4)
대전 사업장 사고 복구 및 기타 일회성 비용(300억원+160억원, 총 460억원) 등이 반영되었다.   
대전 사업장 정상화 시점과 사업 차질 영향은? 
대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 중 정상화될 예정이며, 보은과 여수 공장에서 생산 보완을 위한 조치가 진
행 중인 것으로 소통했다. 또한 대전 사업장은 국내 물량 위주이며, 수출 납기(천무, 천궁-II 추진체 
등)에는 지장이 없을 것이라고 언급했지만, 기타 사업장 램프업을 위한 인건비 증가 등, 수익성 악화는 
불가피할 것으로 예상된다.  
15%대의 매출 성장 가이던스는 달성이 가능할까? 
보수적으로 접근하는 것이 합리적이라는 판단이다. 당사는 ‘26E 지상방산 매출액을 일부 ‘27~28E로 
이연하면서 -6.1% 하향 조정했다. 사측은 폴란드 EC1 천무 인도 가이던스를 기존 40문에서 60문으
로 상향 조정하는 등 납품 속도가 예정대비 빨라진 것을 언급했다. 다만, 천무 EC1에서 미사일 물량 
비중이 높은 것으로 파악되기 때문에 생산 지연 가능성과 수출입 데이터 상 12월 출하 물량의 매출 인
식 이월 가능성을 반영했다. ‘27E는 폴란드 후속 사업과 루마니아 K9, 호주 레드백 등이 양산 페이즈
에 진입하며 성장세가 지속될 전망이다.    
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 1,480,000원 유지 
실적 쇼크와는 별개로, 미국 K9MH 시제, 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등 수주는 서프라이즈라고 판
단된다. 분기말 수주잔고는 35조원(수출 비중 74%, 스페인 미포함)으로 중장기 성장성에 대한 이견은 
없다. 불가피한 분기 실적 변동으로 동사의 미래 가치를 예단하는 것은 시기상조라는 판단이다.

6개월 목표주가 2,000,000

현재주가 
(26.10.21) 
1,417,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회 
◼ 4Q26 지연 물량 인식 및 신규 공장 기여로 실적 개선 전망 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용). 기
존 공장의 높은 가동률을 기반으로 5공장 매출 기여 확대, 미국 생산거점 
확보, 신규 모달리티 진출을 통한 중장기 성장 지속 전망.

3Q26 Review: 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회  
3Q26 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5% yoy, +12.8% qoq), 영업이익 6,401
억원(+1.1% yoy, +9.1% qoq), 영업이익률 42.9%(-7.5%p yoy)를 기록하며 
당사 추정치 및 시장 컨센서스 상회. 5월 1차 파업에 따른 매출 차질(약 
1,500억원) 에도 불구하고, 기존 1~4공장 Full 가동이 지속되며 견조한 실
적 달성. 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대와 및 미국 록빌 공장 매출 인식도 외
형 성장에 기여. 다만, 신규 생산시설 초기 가동비와 제품 믹스 영향으로 
영업이익률은 전년 동기 대비 하락.

4Q26 매출액 1조 5,764억원(+22.6% yoy), 영업이익 7,075억원
(+34.0% yoy) 전망  
4Q26에는 2분기 파업으로 지연된 생산물량의 순차적인 매출 인식과 미국 
록빌 공장 매출 기여, 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대로 실적 개선 예상, 동사
는 신규 생산시설의 매출 가시성 확보를 바탕으로 2026년 매출 성장률 
20%를 무난히 달성할 것으로 전망(연초 가이던스 15~20%).

신규 수주 회복과 포트폴리오 확장에 주목 
미국 의약품 관세정책 불확실성 완화로 지연됐던 수주 협상이 재개되며 신
규 수주 환경 개선 중. 3Q26 약 3억달러의 신규 수주를 확보했으며, 대형 
계약 협상도 구체화되고 있어 2027년 수주 회복 기대. 이는 5공장 가동률 
확대와 중장기 성장 가시성을 높이는 요인. 6공장은 ‘26년말~’27년초 착
공을 목표로 기존 증설 일정 유지. 
폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 1차 공개매수 응모율 87.88%를 기록하며 연내 
인수 완료 추진 중. 인수 완료 시 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업영역이 확대되며 
글로벌 생산거점 및 고객 기반 다변화 기대.

- Overweight(비중확대)

- Neutral(중립) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상 
- Underweight(비중축소)

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 540,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.28) 
285,500 
조선업종

◼ 글로벌 피어 평균 멀티플 하락을 반영하여 목표주가 하향 
◼ 3Q에 산재한 일회성 요인. 원화강세, 조업일수 감소, 임단협 비용 반영 
◼ 모두의 우려에도 불구하고 본업은 안정적으로 천천히 잘 진행되는 중

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 540,000원으로 -32.5% 하향 
목표주가는 28F EPS 42,343원에 글로벌 조선사 피어 평균 12.8배를 적용해 
산출함. 목표주가 하향은 글로벌 경기 불확실성과 중국 조선소 생산캐파 확장
으로 인한 공급과잉 리스크로 전반적인 산업 멀티플이 하락한 것을 반영함. 
다만 가스선 수주 모멘텀과 발전용 엔진의 수주 및 생산 확대 등에 따른 중장
기 성장 유효

3분기는 원래 비수기입니다 
동사의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영
업이익은 8,992억원(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ), OPM 15.6%를 기록. 3분기는 
1)원달러 환율 하락(전분기 대비 평균 -79원) 2)연휴로 인한 조업일수 감소(47
일), 3)임단협 관련 추가 발생 가능 비용 75% 반영)의 영향이 가장 컸음

중장기적인 모멘텀은 여전히 살아있다 
[상선] 중국 공급 확대에 따른 선가 및 수주 경쟁 심화는 부담이나 LNG선 발
주 재개 및 노후선 교체 수요는 긍정적. 동사의 고부가가치선 위주의 수주 전
략 방향은 유지될 예정 [해양] 루야 프로젝트 진행률 인식으로 4분기도 양호
한 이익률 기대, 다만 현재 수주 부진으로 블록, 혹은 LNG선 투입을 통해 매
출 하락을 방어하는 방안도 구상 중 [방산] 키윗과의 미국 합작 조선소를 통해 
콘솔탱커, 로로선 등 지원함 건조를 시작으로 전투함까지 확장 준비 [엔진] 국
가전력망 및 데이터센터향 발전 수요 확대, 하이퍼스케일러 직접협의로 성장
성 강화, 수주 확정 및 추진 물량 기준 28년 1.5GW, 29년 3GW 생산 계획 중 
                                                               (단위: 십억원, %)

Document 276: AI 圈炸了！MIT 让 GPT-6 Astra 直接改写神经网络权重，Qwen数学成绩暴涨6倍

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI 圈炸了！MIT 让 GPT-6 Astra 直接改写神经网络权重，Qwen数学成绩暴涨6倍.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 的实验中， Astra 仅修改约 0.016% 的权重，就将模型的 MATH-500 成绩从
7.9% 提升⾄ 48.1% ， GSM8K 从 13.8% 提升⾄ 67.1% ， IFEval 指令遵循成绩也从 21.0% 提
升⾄ 62.3% 。这意味着，⼤模型开始具备直接理解和编辑神经⽹络参数的能⼒。
摘要：
AI 圈⼜出狠活了！
MIT CSAIL 团队提出了⼀种叫 “ 权重空间编程 ” （ Weight-Space Programming ）的⽅法：不训练、
不算梯度，让 GPT-6 Astra 直接读取和修改神经⽹络的权重。
在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 的实验中， Astra 仅修改约 0.016% 的权重，就将模型的 MATH-500 成绩从 7.9%
提升⾄ 48.1% ， GSM8K 从 13.8% 提升⾄ 67.1% ， IFEval 指令遵循成绩也从 21.0% 提升⾄ 62.3% 。
这意味着，⼤模型开始具备直接理解和编辑神经⽹络参数的能⼒。

这项⼯作由 MIT CSAIL 博⼠⽣ Rikiya Takehi 与 Jayden Teoh 共同领衔，合作者包括 Alex Zhang 和
Omar Khattab 。研究团队将相关成果发布在《 Sparks of Weight-Space Programming 》研究博
客中。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⼀、什么是权重空间编程？
权重是神经⽹络中的核⼼参数，决定模型如何处理输⼊信息，并⽣成最终输出。
传统模型训练通常需要准备数据、计算损失和梯度，再通过优化器不断调整权重。整个过程依
赖⼤量计算，模型内部的参数也难以被⼈类直接理解。
权重空间编程提供了⼀种不同思路：
将神经⽹络的权重视为⼀种可以被⼤模型读取、分析和修改的对象。
通⽤⼤模型直接根据⾃然语⾔⽬标，判断⽹络的功能、定位异常结构，并修改相应参数。
在这项研究中， Astra 主要完成了三类任务： 1. 读取权重，判断神经⽹络被训练来执⾏什么任
务； 2. 修改权重，修复⽹络中的特定故障； 3. 从零⽣成权重，构造具备特定功能的⼩型⽹络。
研究团队⾸先测试了 Astra 理解神经⽹络权重的能⼒。
在第⼀组实验中，研究⼈员使⽤ PPO 训练了四个结构完全相同的⼩型神经⽹络，分别⽤于玩
Breakout 、 Pong 、 Pac-Man 和 Freeway 。

### Page 3

Astra 获得全部原始权重，但不知道候选游戏名称，也不能通过运⾏⽹络观察⾏为。最终，它正
确识别出了四个⽹络各⾃对应的游戏。
在第⼆组实验中，研究⼈员训练了三个扩散模型，分别⽣成圆形、三⻆形和正⽅形。 Astra 同样
只根据权重，成功判断了每个模型的⽣成⽬标。
第三组实验进⼀步扩展到语⾔模型。研究⼈员训练了三个参数量为 720 万的⼩型 Transformer ，
分别学习 Python 代码、罗⻢字⽇⽂和英⽂。即使只提供中间层权重，移除输⼊和输出层， Astra
仍然能够识别训练语料类型，并找出⽹络中的复制注意⼒头。
这些实验表明，通⽤⼤模型可能不仅能够处理⽂本和代码，也能从权重中提取神经⽹络的功能
信息。
⼆、仅修改 0.016% 权重，修复推理循环

### Page 4

Qwen3.5-0.8B 实验是这项研究中最受关注的部分。
该模型在开启思考模式后容易陷⼊推理循环，反复输出相似内容，⽆法及时给出最终答案。基
线测试中， MATH-500 有 93% 的回答触及 32768 个 token 的⻓度上限。
Astra 通过 Python 接⼝访问模型权重，并使⽤ GSM8K 和 MATH 训练集中的练习题检查模型⾏为，
但不能使⽤训练或梯度信息，也不能接触最终测试集。
经过分析， Astra 将问题定位到第 15 层和第 19 层的部分 MLP 结构，并删除了⼀个与重复输出有关
的⽅向。
这次修改共涉及 131072 个权重，约占模型总权重的 0.016% 。修改后， MATH-500 回答触及
token 上限的⽐例从 93% 下降⾄ 33.1% 。
因此，这次成绩提升主要不是向模型注⼊新的数学知识，⽽是修复了阻碍模型完成推理的故
障。部分原本能够解决的问题，在修改后可以正常输出完整答案。
不过，最终补丁虽然很⼩，分析过程并不简单。表现最好的实验使⽤了 50 轮评估请求、 158 轮对

话和 80 次 Python 运⾏，耗时 20.3 ⼩时。
三、从零编写神经⽹络权重
研究团队还测试了 Astra 从零⽣成⽹络参数的能⼒。
在 Breakout 、 Seaquest 和 Asterix 三款 MinAtar 游戏中， Astra 获得游戏规则、源代码和⽹络结
构，但不能使⽤训练数据、损失函数、梯度或环境试玩。
它直接⽣成的新⽹络，在三款游戏中均超过了经过 1 亿步 PPO 训练的对应⽹络。

### Page 5

与训练 5 亿步的 PPO ⽹络相⽐， Astra ⽣成的⽹络在 Breakout 中取得约 20 倍分数，在 Asterix 中表
现接近，在 Seaquest 中达到后者约 60% 的分数。
在 9×9 围棋实验中， Astra ⽣成了⼀个约 148 万参数的⽹络。随着允许使⽤的前向评估次数增加，
⽹络表现继续提升；在 50 次前向评估的条件下，它击败了经过 2000 万局训练的同等规模 PPO ⽹
络。
这些结果说明，⼤模型可能已经能够根据任务描述，将部分算法逻辑直接转换为神经⽹络权
重。
不过，相关实验仍主要集中在结构简单、规模较⼩的⽹络上，能否扩展到更⼤、更复杂的模
型，还需要进⼀步验证。
为了区分 “ 修复故障 ” 和 “ 提升能⼒ ” ，研究团队还在 Gemma 3 270M 上进⾏了追加实验。
Gemma 3 270M 很少出现推理循环，主要错误来⾃算术和代数推理。 Astra 尝试了多轮针对性修
改，但 MATH-500 成绩的提升始终未超过约 1 个百分点，⽆法确认模型获得了稳定改进。

这表明，权重空间编程⽬前更适合处理明确、集中的模型故障，例如异常循环或特定⾏为，⽽
不能直接增加模型原本不具备的知识和推理能⼒。
同时， Astra 在另⼀项实验中未能仅凭权重识别哪个模型经过谄媚⾏为训练，也说明其权重理解
能⼒并不稳定，仍然存在任务边界。
⽬前，这些成果属于研究团队发布的早期结果，尚需更多独⽴复现和系统评估。
如果这种⽅法能够进⼀步扩展，它可能⽤于定位模型故障、删除异常⾏为、提⾼可解释性，甚
⾄根据⾃然语⾔需求⽣成特定⽹络。
但现阶段的实验还不⾜以证明它可以取代训练，也不意味着⼤模型已经能够普遍地 “ ⼿搓 ” 其他模
型。
这项研究更重要的意义在于，它展⽰了⼀种新的交互⽅式：
不只是⽣成⽂本和代码，也开始理解并编写神经⽹络本⾝。

### Page 6

⻛险提⽰及免责条款

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 341: EcoNews of 26 October 2026 (2026-10-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/EcoNews of 26 October 2026 (2026-10-26).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.39 / 26 October 2026ECONEWS
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ADVANCED ECONOMIES
In brief. The week was dominated by a continued global selloff in tech
stocks, leading to safe haven flows into US Treasuries, Japanese Go -
vernment bonds and German bunds. As a result, their yields fell des -
pite energy prices surging to or past their Spring highs. Economic data
generally showed continued resilience. French bonds were under rising

pressure, but Moody's unexpected decision to maintain its France ra -
ting unchanged could, at the margin, help stabilise the spread. The Fed,
ECB and BOJ should be on hold next week while signaling further rate
action is likely.
EUROPEAN UNION
A win for securitization. The Council and the European Parliament have
reached a provisional political agreement on measures to revive and
simplify the EU’s securitization market. The updated framework will

reduce banks’ capital requirements for the safest and most well-struc-
tured transactions, notably for a newly introduced “resilient” category
of securitization. As a key component of the “One Europe, One Market”
initiative and the EU’s Savings and Investment Union agenda, this aims
to channel increased lending to households and businesses. The rele -
vant legal texts must now be finalised.
EUROZONE
Expansion continues. The flash PMIs weakened in October, mainly on

the back of a decline in France. The EZ composite index dipped to 51.9
(-1.2 m/m), dragged down by services (51.7, -1.3 m/m), although ma -
nufacturing also dipped (52.4, -0.5 m/m). Both remain in expansion
territory, and manufacturing new export orders rose to their highest
level since early 2022. Price signals were also rather reassuring, with
the services output price index easing to 54.9 (-1.7 m/m). Households
are more cautious: consumer confidence fell for a second consecutive

month to a five-month low (-17.9). Households’ inflation expectations
edged up at short horizons (+0.1 pp to 3.6% at one year and 3.1% at
three years), but long-term expectations remained anchored, with the
five-year median stable at 2.5%.
France
Rising market stress despite decent economic news; a reprieve from
Moody’s. The spread on French 10-year bond relative to Germany’s
peaked to 174 bps on Friday, the highest since 2011 and only 16 bps
short of its all-time high, as markets price in anticipation of worst-case

scenarios regarding the country’s ability to repair its public finances.
The 10Y yield briefly exceeded 5.1% before falling back to 5% (above
the US equivalent). Nonetheless, services output was stable in August,
following a 0.6% m/m increase in July, a much higher pace than in Q2.
The rebound is driven by the catering sector, the digital sector, business
services, and real estate. The INSEE business climate improved from
96 to 97 (long-term average = 100) in October, but the survey was

conducted before the riots. The main improvement was in manufactu-
ring, which rose from 101 to 105, driven by increased new orders in the
aeronautics sector, and to a lesser extent by the services sector (from
98 to 99). The PMI survey (conducted partially after the riots) was
less positive: the composite index fell to 48.4 (-2.7 m/m), with a sharp
decline in services (48.4; -2.8 m/m) and a slightly smaller decline in
manufacturing (49.3; -1.3 m/m). Moody's unexpectedly announced af -

ter the market close on Friday that it was maintaining France’s ra -
ting at Aa3 on account of France’s diversified economic structure and
sound public institutions, but maintained its negative outlook. This is
on par with the UK and one notch above S&P and Fitch. Moody’s made
clear keeping this rating is conditional on a budget being adopted that
reduces the fiscal deficit, along with an easing of social and market
stress going forward. Saturday’s demonstrations across the country

saw strong attendance but only limited violent incidents.
Germany
Prices continue to rise, but confidence surveys remain on an upward
trend. Producer prices rose 6.7% y/y in September (+2.1pp m/m; the
highest level in 3.5 years) due to higher prices for energy and inter -
mediate goods, primarily metals. The ZEW Economic Sentiment Index
improved beyond consensus forecasts in October and returned to its
highest level since May 2023 (-35.9; +11.2 m/m), albeit the Economic

Expectations Index fell to its lowest level since July (28.1; -6.6 m/m).
The recovery in economic activity continues, although a slowdown is
evident: the flash Composite PMI slowed (52.8; -1 m/m; the lowest in
2 months) due to weaker growth in the services sector (51.7; -1.1 m/m;
the lowest in 2 months) and in manufacturing output (55.1; -0.9 m/m;
the lowest in 3 months). Nevertheless, in both sectors, hiring is rising
slightly, supported by growth in export sales and the spillover effects of

investment plans. Despite persistently high input prices, selling prices
are slowing (reaching their lowest level since February in the manu -
facturing sector).
UNITED KINGDOM
Inflation, wage growth and activity accelerate. CPI inflation overshot
consensus on energy prices, printing at 0.4% m/m and 3.5% y/y in Sep-
tember (consensus: 3.4%, prior: 3.1%), its highest in 11 months. Core
CPI came in at 2.8% y/y (up 0.2 pp), in line with consensus. Pressures

remained persistent according to the PPI at 8.3% y/y (prior: 6.7%). La -
bour market and activity data also surprised positively. Payroll growth
reached 102k in August (3m/3m), bringing the unemployment rate to
4.7% (down 0.2 pp), its lowest since July 2025. October PMI surveys
beat expectations in both manufacturing (52.8, consensus: 51.8, prior:
51.9) and services (53.1, consensus: 51.8, prior: 52.1). The only negative
news came from headline retail sales, falling 0.4% m/m in September,

with the core gauge (excluding petrol) deteriorating by -0.7% m/m.
UNITED STATES
Construction struggling, GENIUS Act implemented. Housing starts
dropped -5.2% m/m in September (-7.4% y/y). This third consecutive
monthly decline dragged the Q3 average to its lowest since Q2 2020.
Building permits also came in negative, at -0.2% m/m (-3.0% y/y), while
the NAHB homebuilder sentiment index plunged to its weakest since
2012. The average 30-year fixed mortgage rate eased to 7.32% (still

134bp above pre-war level), as the ongoing AI selloff pushed down
US treasury yields. The GENIUS Act final implementation rule from the
Office of the Comptroller of the Currency’s (OCC) leaves the door open
for stablecoin customers to receive yield or rewards. Issuers remain
prohibited from paying holders any form of interest or yield solely
in connection with the holding, use, or retention of payment stable -
coins. This represents a shift from the OCC’s February proposal and

has renewed concerns within the banking industry about deposit flight.
The rule will take effect on January 18, 2027.
JAPAN
Activity and inflation keep rising. Composite PMI was unchanged in
September at 52.3, solidly in expansion territory, as an improvement
in services activity offset slower output growth in manufacturing. The
Manufacturing PMI disappointed at 53.3 (-0.8 m/m; an 8-month low).
Underlying price pressures, meanwhile, extended. Headline CPI was

Core CPI (excl. fresh food) matched it at 2.1% y/y (up 0.4 pp), in line
with consensus. More importantly, the New Core index, which strips

EMERGING ECONOMIES

ASIA
China
New monetary policy tool and export controls. The central bank’s Go-
vernor Pan Gongsheng unveiled a plan to switch (“when appropriate”)
its key policy rate from the 7-day reverse repo rate (at 1.4% since May
2025) to the overnight reverse repo rate, in order to reflect develop -
ments in the interbank market. New export controls: The government
plans to add 40 key technologies to the list of technologies prohibited
or restricted for export, including in the AI (for ex. LLMs, computing

clusters), biotechnology and photovoltaic sectors.
Indonesia
BI kept policy rate unchanged at 5.75%, raised its deposit facility rate
by 25bp to 5% and announced a phased cut in reserve requirements
from 9% to 7% by December. This combination of limited FX support
and measures to support credit growth sends mixed signals amidst
pressures on the rupiah following MSCI’s downgrade warning and un-
dermines the central bank’s credibility. The rupiah has lost 1% against

the USD since mid-October, with little contagion effect on other Asian
currencies.
South Korea
The Bank of Korea (BoK) kept its policy rate unchanged at 3.0%, but
the tightening cycle may not be over. CPI inflation was 2.9% y/y in Sep-
tember, above the central bank’s target of 2%, while high oil prices,
rapid growth in Seoul housing prices and wage pressures (though
concentrated in the electronics sector) raise the risk of lasting infla -
tion pressure. As economic activity remains robust, supported by strong

exports and recovering investment, the BoK has scope to raise rates
further.
EMEA
Hungary
Monetary status quo and 2027 budget in consolidation mode. The
Central Bank kept its policy rate unchanged at 5.5%, as expected. A cau-
tious stance will likely prevail in the short term. Inflation has been
picking up in recent months, albeit mildly, and will continue to move
upward as the government recently removed the margin cap on fuel.
Separately, the Budget for 2027 and the medium-term fiscal plan show

Hungary’s goal to join the Eurozone; the government expects to reduce
the fiscal deficit to 4.9% of GDP in 2027 (from 7% expected in 2026) and
towards 2.8% of GDP by 2030.
Türkiye
Cautious monetary loosening. The Central Bank lowered its policy rate
by 100 bp to 36% at its October Monetary Policy Committee meeting,
in line with market expectations. The overnight lending and borrowing
rates were also reduced by 100 bp, to 39% and 34.5%, respectively.

The improvement in underlying inflation has yet to translate into a
sustained period of low inflation, justifying the CBRT’s preference for a
measured pace of monetary easing.
LATIN AMERICA
Brazil
President Lula da Silva was re-elected on Sunday for a fourth term
(with 51.8% of the vote), defeating far right-wing candidate Flavio Bol-
sonaro (48.2%). Lula will enter this new term facing significant econo -
mic and political challenges. Public debt has risen to more than 82% of

GDP , while high borrowing costs and rigid mandatory spending leave
limited room to reconcile fiscal consolidation with social spending and
investment. Restoring confidence in the fiscal outlook will be a key
challenge in the short term.
COMMODITIES
Pressure rising. Amid growing rumours of imminent re-escalation of
military operations in the Gulf, energy prices rose sharply this week,
with brent touching $117/b – near its April peak, and European gas

prices (TTF) rising to 97 €/MWh on Monday, their highest level since
Dec 2022. The Asian gas benchmark (JKM) is following the same trend
(4.6% w/w to 31.4 $/mbtu, equivalent to around 96 €/MWh). Diesel
crack spreads are slightly decreasing in Europe (-2.8% w/w on Monday)
and in the US (-4% w/w on Monday). The first Russian diesel tanker
intended to the US market left Russia this Friday and should supply the
US market in 2 weeks. US diesel average retail prices remain oriented

to the upside (0.7% w/w at 6.32 $/gallon on Monday). US commercial
crude inventories fell by 3.1 mb in the week ending 16 October, signi -
ficantly exceeding market expectations for a 340,000-barrel decline.

(Thursday) and Q3 GDP (Friday). France Household Confidence
(Tuesday), Q3 GDP , October Inflation and Q3 Employment (Friday).

• United States : FOMC (Wednesday) and Q3 GDP (Thursday)
• Japan : October Household Confidence (Thursday), BOJ meeting, Sep-

Document 364: FX Daily_ Democratic sweep can weigh on the dollar

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Democratic sweep can weigh on the dollar.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

We don’t expect today’s US midterm results to cause structural
shifts in FX, but the dollar may face some short-term pressure
should the Democrats secure both the House (widely expected)
and the Senate (close call). Elsewhere, a dovish hike by the
RBA has hit AUD, but we remain optimistic on the outlook for
the currency beyond the near term

Francesco Pesole, Frantisek Taborsky and Chris Turner
We still expect the FX impact from the midterms to be short-lived
USD: Some downside risk if Democrats
win it all
All 435 House of Representatives seats and 35 of the 100 Senate
seats are up for election today in the US. The Democrats are widely
expected to comfortably take the House, where they need a net
gain of just four seats. The Senate is expected to be much closer,
though prediction markets still give the Democrats a 74% chance of
securing it.

### Page 2

We discussed three election scenarios in this note a couple of weeks
ago. Our baseline assumption is that markets fully price in a
Republican loss of the House, but only partially a Democratic clean
sweep. A blue sweep can put some pressure on the dollar. Fears of
tougher regulation could weigh on AI stocks (we’ve recently seen
how that can hit the greenback), while markets could price in
greater risks of decision-making gridlock around issues such as

government funding and the debt ceiling.
Still, we doubt that’s enough to trigger a structural directional shift
in the dollar. The greenback continues to respond predominantly to
the Fed story, which shouldn’t face an immediate and direct impact
from the midterm result. We expect a split Congress (R-S, D-H) to
have a neutral or slightly positive USD impact.
On timing, polls close between midnight and 3.00 AM GMT. US
media outlets may have already called the House for the

Democrats before the London open tomorrow. The tighter Senate
races may take much longer to call, possibly days, partly because
of mail-in ballot counting delays and potential legal disputes. A
close eye will be on seven toss-up Senate races: Alaska, Iowa,
Kansas, Maine, Michigan, Nebraska, Ohio and Texas. In six of those,
the seat is currently held by a Republican.
Elsewhere, oil prices have been the key drivers in FX at the start of
the week. Hopes of a revamp in diplomatic eﬀorts have sent Brent

tumbling back below $105/bl, while the dollar has softened slightly
alongside commodity currencies. On the macro side, today’s
calendar includes US JOLTS job openings.
Francesco Pesole
EUR: ECB speakers have stayed hawkish
EUR/USD is looking to establish support at 1.120 after a good start
to the week. A Democratic sweep at the midterms today could help,
but we don’t expect radical mood changes in the pair.
French spreads didn’t beneﬁt from lower oil prices yesterday,

signalling levels above 150bp are the new normal now. It therefore
remains hard for the euro to shake out the lingering risk premium,

and much more tangible progress is needed for oil to trade

In the eurozone, focus will be on a number of ECB speakers today.
Since last week’s meeting, we’ve heard some hawkish comments,
endorsing market expectations that at least another hike is needed.
Elevated energy prices suggest there is little room for a clear dovish
current to emerge yet.
Francesco Pesole
AUD: Dovish hike doesn't hit Aussie
dollar's medium term outlook

The Aussie dollar came under pressure this morning after the RBA
hiked rates as expected to 4.85%, but delivered some dovish
signals. First, the 6-3 split suggests lower conviction around today’s
decision than in markets, which had almost fully priced in a hike
(21bp). Second, the statement dropped its reference to potential
further hikes for the ﬁrst time since June. Third, the economic
outlook was revised sharply to the downside, with a focus on falling

house prices weighing on consumers and upside risks to
unemployment.
At the press conference, Governor Michele Bullock reinforced the
RBA’s wait-and-see approach, stressing that monetary tightening
still has to feed through to the economy. She left the door open to
further tightening, but the bar is clearly higher now. The RBA
appears happy to assess the impact of previous hikes before acting
again.
This is in line with our forecast of no further RBA hikes. Despite

today’s correction, we remain positive on AUD, which oﬀers the
best short-term carry in G10, including on a volatility-adjusted
basis. Markets price only a one-in-three chance of another hike at
the coming meetings and already embed 13bp of easing by the
end of 2027, leaving AUD with limited risk of further dovish repricing
for now. The current environment remains a bit challenging for AUD
amid AI-related jitters, but we remain conﬁdent that our 0.710

AUD/USD target can be reached by year-end.

### Page 4

Francesco Pesole
CEE: Zloty is the weakest link in the
region
Yesterday’s PMI readings in the region conﬁrmed the economy's
strong position across the board. The outlook remains positive.
With the exception of Turkey, all countries show PMI levels higher
than those seen before the onset of the US-Iran conﬂict. The Czech
Republic and Hungary appear particularly strong, hovering near or
reaching new highs; Poland is edging higher, while Romania has

declined once again. Overall, however, the economy continues to
show resilience, pointing to a stable outlook for the coming
months.
Today, Turkey’s inﬂation data for October is released. We expect
monthly inﬂation to accelerate from 1.8% to 2.4%, driven by
increased energy cost pressures, which the Central Bank of Turkey
has identiﬁed as an upside risk, as well as seasonal rises in food
and clothing prices. This would result in a slight decline in annual

inﬂation, from 29.7% in September to 29.6%. This should be
suﬃcient to allow for at least one more rate cut by the CBT this
year.
EUR/PLN tested new highs yesterday following political headlines
over the weekend. In our view, these developments provide further
bearish momentum, suggesting additional downside for the zloty.
Naturally, global conditions remain unfavourable, characterised by
a strong US dollar and high energy prices, despite some
improvement yesterday. Furthermore, Poland’s public ﬁnance

deﬁcit, the highest in the EU, leaves the country exposed to
potential turmoil in the euro-denominated bond market.
Additionally, we anticipate a dovish tone from the National Bank of
Poland at this week’s meeting. Taken together, we continue to view
the zloty as the weakest link in the CEE region and expect the
EUR/PLN pair to settle above 4.420.
Frantisek Taborsky

► 美國經濟仍具韌性 ，惟通膨壓力猶存 。觀察
美國9月ISM服務業指數表現 ，內需活動維持
穩健，企業投入成本壓力亦有所升溫；另一
方面，受地緣政治因素影響 ，美國柴油價格
大幅攀升 ，而柴油為卡車運輸 、農業及製造
業的重要燃料 ，若價格持續維持高檔 ，恐進
一步增添通膨壓力 。考量聯準會偏鷹立場 ，
且當前通膨降溫進程不如預期 ，年底前仍保
有升息空間。
► 美國債務總額突破40兆美元，預估2026年公
眾持有聯邦債務占 GDP的比率將達 101%，
財政可持續性風險升溫 。與此同時 ，AI基礎

建設舉債熱潮 ，疊加中東局勢動盪帶來的通
膨不確定性 ，迫使投資人重新定價長天期債
券風險 ，要求更高的風險溢酬 。隨實質殖利
率上行，美債10年期殖利率揚升並持續高懸
於5%水準之上，30年期長債殖利率亦同步站
上近20年來高點 。隨長天期債券利率續揚 ，
殖利率曲線趨陡峭 ，債券配置宜先避開長天
期部位，聚焦中短天期債券鎖利機會。

► 距離美國期中選舉投票僅剩不到1個月，觀察
Polymarket賭盤預估數據顯示 ，眾議院選情

院。但參議院選情則尚有變數 ，今年以來兩
黨均互有領先 ，雖然數據顯示民主黨掌控參
議院機率呈現上揚 ，然隨時序進入選前最後
衝刺期 ，參議院能否亦由民主黨掌控仍待觀
察。故就選前而言 ，由於不同政黨的政策傾
向差異 ，潛在受惠產業亦有不同 ，選舉結果
的不確定性恐令投資人不易大舉押注股市。

投資焦點日股
► 9月日本銀行決議升息 1碼，明確表示潛在
CPI已接近2%，未來也將繼續調高利率 ，政
策重點開始轉向防範通膨向上偏離目標 。然
因有2位委員反對升息，加上總裁植田和男會
後談話偏向中性 ，令市場解讀此次升息未如
預期鷹派，美日利差不易快速縮窄，故9月會
議後日元反而呈現利多出盡的回貶反應。
► 觀察今年以來日股面臨短線日圓升值的狀態
並未出現顯著走弱 態勢。一方面反映企業治
理改革推動股東報酬 提升已成為 支撐股價的
重要結構性因素 。另一方面 ，升息代表日本
經濟已具備一定的健康程度 ，貨幣政策正常
化將不致對需求帶來衝擊 。因此 ，隨BoJ延
續升息方向 ，將擴大銀行淨利差 ，改善其獲
利結構，並提升金融資產收益 。儘管近期整
體日股預期獲利成長走勢略顯停滯 ，然日本
銀行股 在總 體經 濟 與貨幣 政策環 境的 支持
下，獲利成長有望逆勢攀升 ，料將成為後續
資金關注重點。
日圓升值對日股影響似乎有限，關注銀行股獲利走強BoJ升息方向延續，惟其步伐仍取決於經濟與物價

BoJ政策保持正常化路線，日本銀行股有望持續受惠
投資焦點

### Page 8

8
股市
 公債殖利率走高雖可能壓抑 股債市場近期 動能，然而觀察美股第三季獲利年增 率有望仍為雙位數成
長，或支撐股市表現 。市場持續關注AI資本支出是否帶來獲利成長 ，短期內評價面壓力將增添市場
動盪，可分散配置於不同國家與產業 ，降低市場波動影響 。總結上述，地緣衝突、殖利率偏高及評
價面壓力等紛擾 帶來干擾，可布局防禦 股增強抗震力 。長期而言，科技及AI半導體發展趨勢不變且
基本面佳，可逢拉回時分批布局。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，加上全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍
工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融及醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，且最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示 ，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升息空間 ，立場
偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨 發展。殖利率偏高環境建議以中短天期債券投資為主 ，可逢利率走
升時布局進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸

引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期的鷹派，日圓震盪。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期紛擾難解，偏高的油價可能對通膨及實體經濟帶來影響。
 短期受美國雙率走高有壓 ，惟美日財政赤字壓力與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊 ，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 短期抗波動，逢回可分批布局仍具長線趨勢之AI與科技產業

### Page 15

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

United States: Elevated inflation and high growth
02/10/2026 4
August 2026: Marked improvement in services
business confidence, from an already high level
September 2026: Manufacturing business

confidence remains stable at a high level
August 2026: Increasing inflationary pressures September 2026: Supplier deliveries do not get
longer
August 2026: Consumers are a bit more
concerned about their personal finances
August 2026: Headline inflation resists to the
upside
U.S. macroeconomic performance has remained solid since the onset of the shock, as illustrated in particular by Q2 growth
of +3.8% and +9% (annualized quarterly rate) in consumer spending and private nonresidential investment, respectively.

In the manufacturing sector, business sentiment has gained momentum despite the shock, while the impact of this shock
on the services sector has remained very limited. The immediate reaction of consumer confidence was, however, more
negative before beginning to improve, which was nevertheless interrupted in August (latest available data for households’
sentiment on their future personal financial situation). The University of Michigan’s composite indicator continued to

deteriorate in September, due to the sharp rise in fuel prices. But declining confidence still does not translate into reduced
spending—at least not at the aggregate level.
On the inflation front, the spike immediately following the outbreak of the conflict in Iran was more pronounced than in
the months following Russia’s invasion of Ukraine. The decline in June still left inflation at a high level, but its stability in
July and August was notable (3.4% y/y), as was the latest—the third consecutive—slight decline in inflation excluding

energy in August (2.5% y/y, same rate as in February). Given the existing inflationary pressures, however, inflation is highly
likely to resume its upward trend in September.
Source: BLS, ISM, NFIB, University of Michigan, BNP Paribas

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
2
EXECUTIVE SUMMARY
• The Westpac–DataX Card Tracker
Index* has edged down slightly since
late September, slipping 0.9pts to 160.6
in the week ended October 17. Despite
this, growth in activity continues to track
a robust pace with quarterly momentum

running at close to 2%qtr and annual
growth at 6%yr.
• Some of this reflects revisions. Initial
figures on card activity can see changes
in subsequent weeks due to information
lags. While these are usually small,
the cumulative impact can sometimes
be material. In this case, revisions have
lifted the index in late September by
2.5pts and the 4wk trend growth rate at
the time from 0.2% to 0.6%. We will be
back-testing regular revisions to assess
whether there is a systematic bias that

can be adjusted for in future.
• The revised profile shows quarterly
growth accelerating steadily since
June, posting a 1.9%qtr gain for the
September quarter and tracking a
2.1%qtr pace in the latest week. This is
back near the robust pace seen in late
2024 and early 2025.
• The monthly and weekly reads show
a softer 0.2%mth rise in August was
followed by a solid 0.8%mth gain in
September that has largely carried into
the first two weeks of October (the latest

observation tracking a 0.4%mth pace).
• The detail shows gains are not just
due to higher fuel prices with strong
growth in discretionary services and
international transactions over the last
quarter and steady growth in essential
services as well. To the extent that the
weekly and monthly data is showing
some renewed squeeze from higher fuel
prices, this is showing through most
clearly in discretionary goods, which
have seen activity decline over the last

six weeks, with discretionary services
still posting positive, albeit slower
growth. Quarterly growth in non-fuel
card activity is tracking around 1.4%qtr.
• Official September quarter figures on
inflation and household spending due
in the coming weeks should confirm the
spending momentum and shed more
light on the extent to which it is due to
prices or volumes.

running at close to 2%qtr ...”

• Chart 2 shows how quarterly growth
in the Westpac–DataX Card Tracker
Index compares to growth in nominal

• Chart 4 shows quarterly growth in card
activity broken down by contributions
from major state and category groups.
• The contribution from essential goods
captures the effect of fuel price swings.
Outside of this, discretionary services

of NSW and Vic account for nearly two

significant revisions. Data lags and
cleaning often result in shifts to historical
estimates. These are typically small but
can be have a material cumulative effect.

revision: for the last week of September, it
lifts the WCTI 2.5pts and the 4wk trend
growth rate from 0.2% to 0.6%, removing
what looked to be a slowing in momentum
at the time. We will be investigating this

issue more closely in coming weeks to see
whether there is a systematic bias that
can be adjusted for in the future.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS 5
• Our MHSI and retail card indexes 
are composite measures based on 
transactions in categories that are in 
scope for the ABS monthly household 
spending indicator (MHSI) and retail 
sales estimates (which have been based 
on the MHSI detail since the ABS retail 
survey was discontinued in June 2025). 
• After under-performing the broader 
WCTI in Q2, the MHSI index has moved 
more in line in Q3, posting growth of 
1.6%qtr with the latest week running  at 
2%qtr. The retail card index has been 
more subdued, up 1.1%qtr in Q3, growth 
running at just 0.4%qtr in the latest week.
• The ABS MHSI has been firmer than 
our index since June, mainly reflecting 
strong gains in vehicle sales which are 
not incorporated into our index. Vehicle 
sales appear to have been relatively flat in 
recent months, our estimates suggesting 
the official measure, due Nov 4, should 
show quarterly growth more in line with 
our indicator (Chart 7).
• The more granular detail on the retail 
card index shows non-food retail 
contracting materially over the last four 
weeks, consistent with the weakness 
being observed across discretionary 
goods categories.

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
WESTPAC ECONOMICS 6
• The Westpac Card Tracker draws on
a rich dataset that sheds light on many
aspects of consumer demand. In some
cases, it can also reveal some nuances
about the way spend is captured – in
official statistics, industry measures and
the card data itself. Vehicles provide a

particularly good example of this.
• Vehicle spend has long been a
bellwether for the wider consumer cycle.
As a large discretionary expenditure, it is
more sensitive to interest rate changes,
‘wealth effects’ and sentiment shifts,
particularly around job security. Given
this, ABS measures and industry figures
on vehicle sales have delivered material
upside surprises over the last year.
Vehicle purchases recorded a remarkable
10.3%qtr surge in the June quarter to be

up 7.3%yr with industry figures showing
vehicle sales holding around these levels
in the September quarter. Even allowing
‘extenuating circumstances’ driving
strong demand for EVs, the results seem
to fly in the face of wider interest rate,
cost-of-living and sentiment narratives.
• The card tracker data allows for a deeper
dive. Note that credit and debit cards are
not often used to purchase vehicles with
account transfers or financing typically

used for the main purchase instead.
• However, cards are used for up-front
payments. This shows through in
payment sizes, which are in the hundreds
of dollars (average $750) not the tens of
thousands. As such, card activity should
still give some guide, particularly around
sales volumes.
• Chart 8 shows how transactions have
tracked and highlight another important
point. Most of the data in this space
focuses on new vehicles (for the ABS
this is because used sales are a transfer

between households that ‘cancels out’ for
the sector as a whole). However, industry
estimates show used sales are both more
common and have been tracking a weaker
profile – the card data suggests used sales
are down at least 3%yr (finer estimates
putting the figure closer to 8%yr).
• Age-groups offer another insightful lens.
Activity has been much firmer across
older age groups, particularly in the ‘new
vehicle’ segment where they are more
prominent (accounting for 42% of all

activity) and activity is up on a year ago.
This group has notably stronger balance
sheets and is low exposure to interest
rate rises. In contrast, vehicle spend has
been contracting materially across other
age-groups, particularly used car spend
by the ‘mortgage belt’ 35-54 year olds.
Vehicle-related card activity highlights diverging trends

WESTPAC ECONOMICS 10
About the Westpac card data indicators
The indicators presented in this report are based on the millions of credit and debit card
transactions processed every day. Transactions covering over ten million merchants are
classified into over 700 categories. These are in turn grouped into higher level aggregates

that provide a timely guide to wider economic trends.
The main focus of these indicators is consumer spending. Where possible, we have sought
to exclude ‘non spending’ transactions such as: money transfers; tax payments; loan
repayments; charitable donations; and superannuation contributions.
It should also be noted that these indicators will also be affected by shifts between card and
non-card transactions. This was a significant factor during the COVID–19 pandemic – health

concerns about the use of physical cash leading to significantly higher use of cards vs cash,
particularly where contact–less transactions are available. Transaction flows also include
reversals/refunds which were also a significant phenomenon during the onset of COVID,
especially in areas such as travel.
All transaction data is compiled at a highly aggregated level so that individual customer or
merchant data is never revealed.
Index construction

The key metrics used in this report are indexes of spending–related card activity where the
base of 100 is average activity in 2019. As an example, if transaction flows are 5% above their
average level in 2019, the index read for the period is 105. If flows in a subsequent period are
8% above the average level in 2019, the index read for this period is 108. Growth between the
two periods can be calculated simply as the change between the two index reads, i.e. 2.9%.

All measures are adjusted for regular seasonality. Weekly estimates are generated using
the US Bureau of Labor’s MoveReg weekly seasonal adjustment program. Note that in some
cases, high levels of volatility during the COVID period mean it is not possible to produce
seasonally adjusted estimates for some historical periods.
Also, note that previous versions of this report used different approaches to seasonal
adjustment and measurement more generally. This means Index reads are sometimes not

directly comparable. See the ‘About the Westpac card data indicators’ sections from earlier
reports to more detail.
Classifications
Note that the measures used for card data and in this report do not align completely with
the those used in official ABS statistics, including the ABS household spending indicator,
ABS retail trade survey and ABS estimate of consumer spending published in the national
accounts. There are a range of differences including around both coverage and classification.

As such, the card data should be treated as broadly indicative.
The transaction data is grouped into 26 categories that are then combined into four main as
follows:
Discretionary goods: alcoholic beverages, tobacco, clothing & footwear, furnishings &
household equipment, household appliances, vehicle–related, recreational & cultural goods,
newspaper, books & stationery, and other personal effects.
Discretionary services: transport services (part), recreational & cultural services, gambling,

catering services, accommodation services, other personal care, insurance & financial
services, other services.
Essential goods: food & non alcoholic beverages, medical products, appliances & equipment,
and operation of personal transport equipment.
Essential services: electricity, gas & other fuels, health services, transport services (part),
communications and education.
The report also uses two additional classifications:
MHSI/Retail: based on the extent to which categories cover activity that is in scope for the

ABS monthly household spending indicator and ABS retail trade survey.

Document 489: ‘Cost-Plus’ Costs (2026-11-04)

Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #labour #rba #productivity
‘Cost-Plus’ Costs
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
10:40 October 16 2026 Share
Entrenched expectations that cost increases can and should be passed on are precisely
what tight monetary policy is leaning against, but only indirectly.
The recent tendency of some firms to bundle into prices card-acceptance costs that had previously
been surcharged – and then some, in some cases – demonstrates a ‘cost-plus’ approach to pricing.

### Page 2

The recent banning of card surcharges has caused considerable upset this month, even though it is a
reversion to historical and international practice. The card schemes used to impose “no-surcharge”
rules, until the RBA banned these in 2003, and many other countries still have them. The turnaround
represents the RBA throwing in the towel on its longstanding effort to use price incentives to get people
to choose low-cost payment methods. Surcharges no longer made sense in a largely cashless
economy. In any case, least-cost routing puts the power to choose lower-cost methods in the hands of
the merchants who actually bear the cost.

In most cases, firms will simply roll the surcharge into the sticker price, leaving the total cost to the
consumer unchanged, just more transparent. We also understand that some large retailers have
absorbed the cost – if they were surcharging in the first place, which many did not. (The RBA estimated
that around 16% of businesses imposed surcharges.) Inevitably, though, some firms have lifted their
prices by more than any reasonable estimate of the merchant service fee that the surcharge was
meant to cover. And because this is Australia, of course takeaway coffee has been the centre of
consumer discontent.

Higher prices for one’s caffeine fix was unlikely to be the main instigator of this month’s slump in
consumer sentiment. Petrol prices are typically more salient for cost-of-living concerns, and the before-
and-after picture on the RBA announcement that week shows that higher interest rates also contributed
to the decline.

In some senses, though, the post-surcharge prices and the RBA’s decisions are linked: businesses
passing on increased costs into their own prices – and expecting to be able to make that increase stick
– is exactly the kind of inflationary environment the RBA is leaning against. Firms have been subjected
to an array of non-labour cost escalations, from pandemic-era supply chain issues to electricity to fuel
to water to building supplies. And IT equipment prices, which for decades were a reliable source of cost
reductions, are nowadays flat to up. Passing these costs on, leaving margins intact, demonstrates that
demand is not weak enough to prevent this, and signals that firms’ inflationary expectations might be
higher than the RBA desires. The RBA will take this as a sign that demand is still outstripping supply.

To the extent they can pass on these costs through to prices, it suggests that demand was strong
enough to bear it. The RBA will take this as a signal that demand is still outstripping supply.
This is mostly a story about non-labour costs. Base wages growth has been moderate for some
years, though the above-average increase in award wages this year will add a little. Not all firms will
seek to pass these cost increases on, but some will, including because they have limited scope to
absorb the cost increase into margins.
A cost-plus mentality has drawbacks, including that it limits the imagination away from finding
alternative, efficiency-improving responses. It also accords with recent academic research
suggesting that firms form expectations and set prices according to what is happening in their own
industry and experience, while putting little weight on economy-wide inflation or central bank
credibility. This is an unpleasant message for many consumers or anyone in an interest-sensitive
sector when supply shocks abound.

### Page 3

This is mostly a story about non-labour costs. While the Fair Work Commission did hand down an
above-average increase in award wages this year and this was probably passed through to prices in
hospitality and other labour-intensive sectors, base wages growth has been moderate for some years.
Business surveys point to non-labour cost escalation being the real issue lately. This is why we were
puzzled by the passage in the RBA minutes this week suggesting that cost pressures reported in
business surveys might imply that the unemployment rate consistent with inflation at target (the NAIRU)
has risen. It is a convenient belief, though, if unemployment is starting to overshoot your forecasts, as
the latest labour market data have done compared with the RBA’s August forecasts.

Not all firms can pass on cost increases. Most small firms do not have the market power, though coffee
may be an exception because convenience induces a kind of local market power. And as noted above,
large firms with everyday-low-price strategies will instead try to find savings elsewhere in the supply
chain.

Still, some firms will seek to pass these cost increases on. Some observers might see this as corporate
greed, but another way to frame this strategy is simply as a ‘cost-plus’ mentality. It turns out that cost-
plus pricing is especially common in areas where regulators determine the price – water and other
utilities being examples where costs and prices have escalated a lot in recent years.

There are limits to firms’ ability to absorb costs, too. The failures of so many building firms a few years
ago, squeezed between post-pandemic cost escalation, delivery delays and fixed-price contracts, were
a case in point. Normally, there is a contractual response, and it need not end in insolvency. The
episode does illustrate, however, that it is unrealistic to expect firms never to pass cost increases
through to prices.

The cost-plus mentality does have its drawbacks. It limits the imagination away from finding responses
that might save costs elsewhere to absorb the cost hike, and in so doing, boost efficiency. It was
equally limiting in times of soft demand: in the years before the pandemic, many firms professed a
belief that, because they couldn’t get price increases to stick, they could not possibly accept cost
increases, including in wages. Thus, Australia ended up with wages growth too low to be consistent
with inflation at target, and the peculiar spectacle of a central bank governor complaining that wages
growth was too low. And if labour remains cheap, why invest to economise on it and thereby boost
productivity?

A cost-plus mentality also amplifies the pass-through of supply shocks. This is not quite the same thing
as the ‘inflation expectations’ seen in standard economic models of inflation, but it is related. The
stronger the tendency towards cost-plus pricing, the worse the inflation response to a supply shock.
This is also true when inflation expectations are high, but the distinction matters. The recent academic
literature includes some fascinating empirical evidence from New Zealand that suggests that beliefs
about current and future overall inflation have little bearing on many firms’ pricing decisions. As related
work using French data shows, firms’ pricing choices are shaped less by economy-wide inflation
developments than by what is happening to their costs and in their industry.

### Page 4

Related articles
That adds up to economy-wide behaviour that looks a lot like firms (in aggregate) setting prices
according to expected inflation (in aggregate), but under the hood something quite different is going on.
If firms’ beliefs about future economy-wide inflation do not matter much in their pricing decisions, then
neither does the credibility of a central bank’s commitment to return inflation to target. The mechanism
to get inflation down then must be simply to weaken demand enough to convince firms that cost-plus
will not fly in the market. This is an unpleasant message for many consumers or anyone in an interest-
sensitive sector when supply shocks abound. It does, however, imply that if supply shocks fade or even
self-correct, so will high inflation. If and when that happens, tight monetary policy can be unwound.

The deeper question is whether cost-plus price dynamics can be reduced. That is more likely to be a
question for regulators than for central banks.

《類比晶片終端需求上升 ，工業、資料中心占比較

德州儀器(TXN)3Q26營收60.6億美元、EPS 2.62美
元雙雙優於預期，4Q26財測營收58-63億美元，中
位數較市場普遍預期高出 6%，反映資料中心市場
需求持續成長 ，使營收預測優於往年季節性回落 。
以3Q26終端市場來看 ，工業營收年增超過 40%、
季增約10%，成長來自航太國防 、能源基礎設施等
領域；車用營收年增約 15%、季增約10%，受益於
客戶庫存水準降低及電動車占比提高；資料中心營
收則年增約95%、季增20%，為成長最快的終端市
場。在營運方面，產能利用率提高以及2027年潛在
的價格調漲可能進一步擴大獲利成長空間 ，儘管資

本支出可能高於2026年的20~30億美元，但顯示終
端需求正擴大成長。
恩智浦(NXPI)3Q26營收37.9億美元、EPS 4.20美元
優於預期 ，但4Q26財測營收中位數 38.5億美元略
低於市場預期 。以3Q26終端市場來看 ，車用營收
20.7億美元、年增13%，成長主要來自軟體定義汽
車(SDV)產品貢獻；工業與物聯網營收 8.02億美元 、
年增39%，主要由資料中心 、工廠自動化和醫療保
健等產業帶動；通訊基礎設施營收 5.03億美元、年
增54%，得益於資料中心業務成長 。恩智浦財報結
果顯示各終端市場需求持續上升 ，惟成長速度較慢
的車用營收占比超過五成 ，未來成長速度可能不及
工業和資料中心營收占比更高的同業。

4
《券商 CIO 調查顯示軟體支出預期持續增溫》
券商 CIO 調查指出 2027 年的企業 IT 採購預算仍維
持成長。CIO 現在預計 2026 年的 IT 採購預算將年
增 4.0%，自 2Q26 走高 20bps，連續 3 次增溫。
對於 2027 年 IT 採購預算的首次估計，CIO 預期年
增 4.0%，雖僅與更新後的 2026 年預期相符，但實
則是近 4 年來的開年新高。在這之中，軟體預算成
長優於領先硬體、網通與服務，預期年增 4.4%，且

自 2026 年加速 10bps。2027 年軟體支出的預期加
速可能進一步說明短期內 AI 並未帶來全面性衝擊。
資料來源：摩根史丹利，2026/10/12，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/20
人工智慧與機器學習 (AI/ML) 的支出優先性仍穩居
CIO 調查的第一名，佔比雖較前幾期調研回落，但
這應該是因為 CIO 對於開始增加其他項目的優先性。
AI 相關專案的預算仍最不可能被刪除，且淨占比
(即最不可能被刪除 – 最可能被刪除的比例)，3Q26
較 2Q26 大幅走揚 13ppts 至 17%。網路安全的支
出優先性則連 4 季走揚，且最不可能被刪除的淨占
比也走揚 1ppt 至 16%。持續反映出 AI 帶動的攻擊
面擴大、攻擊手法更加多元，與攻擊成本大幅走低，
導致企業在 AI 顯著產生正面效益前，優先選擇防範
未然的心態。而隨著 AI 的能力仍在快速發展，如近
期 Anthropic 研究即指出 AI 已可自我迭代，且小
型模型未來可能具備在物聯網裝置上自我複製的能

力，本行預期網安的需求可望仍持續強健。數位轉
型雖仍處第 3，但在本次券商調查中大幅地低於過
硬體 軟體 通訊設備 IT服務

[インド]～９月インフレ率は２年弱ぶりの高い伸びに、コアインフレ率も加速するなどインフレ圧力が強まる～ 
12 日に発表された９月の消費者物価は前年同月比＋5.54％となり、 前月 （同＋4.82％） から加速
して 2024 年10 月以来の高い伸びとなった。前月比は＋0.69％と前月（同＋0.73％）からわずかに
上昇ペースが鈍化したものの、 原油価格が再び上昇していることを反映してエネルギー価格の上昇
が続いているほか、 生鮮品をはじめとする食料品価格も上昇しており、 生活必需品を中心とするイ
ンフレが続いている。食料品とエネルギーを除いたコアインフレ率は前年同月比＋4.33％となり、
前月（同＋4.16％）から加速した。前月比は＋0.40％と前月（同＋0.54％）から上昇ペースが鈍化
したものの、 エネルギー価格の上昇に伴う輸送コストの増加に加え、 金融市場でのルピー安による
輸入インフレも重なり、幅広い財で価格が上昇している。さらに、サービス物価も上昇基調で推移
しており、インフレ圧力が広がっている様子がうかがえる。

### Page 2

2/4

[オーストラリア]～大都市部を中心に雇用に変調、労働市場への参入意欲にも陰りの兆し～ 
15 日に発表された９月の失業率 （季調済） は4.7％となり、 前月 （4.7％） から２ヵ月連続で横ば
いとなった。失業者数は前月比＋0.9 万人と前月（同＋2.8 万人）から４ヵ月連続で増加した。雇
用形態別では、パートタイムの求職者数（同▲0.9 万人）が２ヵ月ぶりの減少に転じる一方、フル
タイムの求職者数 （同＋1.8 万人） は４ヵ月連続で増加しており、 失業者数全体を押し上げている。
一方、雇用者数は前月比▲3.9 万人と前月（同＋2.9 万人）から２ヵ月ぶりの減少に転じた。雇用
形態別では、フルタイムの雇用者数（同＋3.4 万人）が２ヵ月ぶりの増加に転じる一方、パートタ
イムの雇用者数（同▲7.4 万人）が減少に転じ、雇用全体を押し下げた。地域別では、資源関連産
業が集積する西オーストラリア州で雇用の増加基調が続いているものの、 最大都市シドニーを擁す
るニュー・サウス・ウェールズ州や、第２の都市メルボルンを擁するヴィクトリア州では雇用が減
少するなど、大都市部を中心に雇用環境に変化の兆しがみられる。さらに、労働力人口は前月比▲
3.0 万人と前月 （同＋5.7 万人） から２ヵ月ぶりの減少に転じた。 労働参加率も66.8％と前月 （67.0％）
から 0.2pt 低下しており、 雇用環境の悪化を背景に、 労働市場への参入意欲にも陰りが出つつある
様子がうかがえる。

[韓国]～高齢層で雇用に頭打ちの一方、若年層に持ち直しの動き、雇用環境に変化の兆し～ 
16 日に発表された９月の失業率 （季調済） は2.6％となり、 前月 （2.7％） から0.1pt 改善した。
失業者数は前月比▲4.6 万人と前月（同▲3.3 万人）から２ヵ月連続で減少しており、年代別では
20 代や 30 代のほか、50 代や 60 代以上など幅広い年代で減少する動きがみられる。一方、雇用者

代といった若年層のほか、30 代でも増加ペースが加速しているうえ、60 代以上でも増加が続いて
いる。雇用形態別では、正規雇用者を中心に増加する一方、非正規雇用者は減少するなど、対照的
な動きがみられる。こうしたなか、労働力人口は前月比▲0.1 万人と前月（同＋0.5 万人）から４
ヵ月ぶりの減少に転じた。年代別では、10 代や30 代などで増加する一方、50 代を中心に減少する
動きがみられ、 中高年層の一部で労働市場からの退出が進んでいる可能性がうかがえる。 その一方、
これまで厳しい状況が続いてきた10 代や20 代など若年層の雇用環境には持ち直しの動きがみられ
ており、年代によって雇用情勢に違いが現れつつある。

[シンガポール]～世界的な AI・半導体需要の旺盛さが景気と外需を押し上げる展開が続く～ 
14 日に発表された７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成長率 （速報値） は前年同期比＋5.4％となり、 前期 （同
＋5.9％） から伸びが鈍化した。 前期比年率ベースの成長率も＋4.42％と前期 （同＋6.15％） から鈍
化したものの、引き続きプラス成長を維持するなど、景気は堅調に推移している。分野別の動きを
みると、 世界的なＡＩ （人工知能） ・半導体関連需要の旺盛さを反映して、 電子部品関連のほか、 精
密機械関連や一般機械関連の生産は堅調に推移した。 一方、 化学製品関連や医薬品関連の生産低迷
が重しとなり、製造業全体の生産は伸び悩んだ。また、ＡＩ・半導体関連需要の旺盛さを受けた設
備投資やデータセンター関連投資の活発化に加え、 公共投資の進捗を反映して、 建設業の生産は上
振れしている。さらに、小売・卸売関連のほか、運輸・貿易関連、金融関連など幅広いサービス業
で生産が拡大しており、景気拡大を下支えしている様子がうかがえる。 
16 日に発表された９月の非石油輸出額は前年同月比＋68.4％となり、 前月 （同＋46.1％） から伸
びが加速した。前月比も＋25.53％と前月（同＋10.78％）から２ヵ月連続で拡大しており、持ち直
しの動きを強めている。 化学製品関連や医薬品関連のほか、 食料品関連の輸出に下押し圧力がかか
ったものの、 半導体など電子部品関連や精密機械関連のほか、 鉄鋼製品関連や鉱物資源関連の輸出
は旺盛に推移している。 原油関連を含む総輸出額も前年同月比＋51.5％となり、 前月 （同＋49.2％）
から伸びが加速した。前月比も＋5.1％と前月（同＋3.1％）から４ヵ月連続で拡大しており、単月
ベースの輸出額は過去最高を更新した。 一方、輸入額は前年同月比＋49.9％となり、前月（同＋
39.3％） から伸びが加速した。 前月比も＋12.4％と前月 （同▲0.3％） から２ヵ月ぶりの拡大に転じ
ており、 輸出と同様に持ち直しの動きをみせている。 輸出の堅調さを背景に、 設備機械関連や素材 ・

台北富邦銀行 
Oct 28, 2026 | 16:00PM (GMT+8) 
主要產業 總評： 美股待估值合理  
投資研究部 
 Research 
Financial Market 
▎AI 支出支撐美國經濟高檔不墜 
AI 投資熱潮持續發酵 ， 成為美國經濟擴張的重要動能 。
隨著美國大型科技企業加速佈局人工智慧 ， 帶動資料中
心 、 伺服器及相關軟硬體設備投資顯著成長 ，AI 相關資
本支出對美國實質 GDP 成長的貢獻持續提升，2025 年
第 1 季以來 ， 相關投資對於經濟成長的拉抬效果持續顯
著(圖 1)。 此外 ， 參考NY Fed 預估 ， 美國2026Q4 GDP
季比年增率將由 Q3 的 2.67%、 進一步加速至2.82%，
維持在疫情後相對高位。參考 BofA 預估，2027 年美
國 5 大 CSP 廠 1 資本支出將增加至 1.113 兆美元，較
2026 年的 7930 億增加約 40%，在美國大型 CSP 廠明
年仍預計持續加大資本支出的狀況下 ， 預期美國經濟仍
具韌性 ，有助降低美國景氣動能短期大幅放緩的風險 。

因此，資料中心需求動能，將是美國經濟能否維持強勢
擴張的關鍵因素之一。觀察北美資料中心在建規模，並
對照空置率 ，代表資料中心供給量的在建規模持續增加 ，
但空置率卻自 2023H2 以來持續下滑 ， 截至2026H1，
北美資料中心空置率降至 1.4%(圖 2)，為北美 REITs 中
少數基本面持續改善的次產業之一 ， 顯示資料中心仍處
於極度供不應求的狀況。 
註 1：美國 5 大 CSP 廠為 MSFT*、AMZN*、GOOGLE*、META*、
ORCL* 
圖 1 AI 對 GDP 貢獻自 2025Q1 以來維持高檔

圖 2 北美資料中心空置率持續下滑

資料來源：方正證券, 2026/9/16 
 ◼ AI 投資熱潮持續支撐美國經濟 ， 科技巨頭加速擴建資料中心 ， 帶動資本支出對GDP
貢獻提升。參考 NY Fed 最新預估，美國第 4 季經濟成長率將由第 3 季的 2.67%
升至 2.82%。五大雲端服務商 2027 年資本支出有望達 1.113 兆美元，年增約四
成。北美資料中心空置率降至 1.4%，凸顯算力需求強勁，支撐美國景氣韌性。  
◼ 美國通膨尚未全面擴散，核心 CPI 年增率有望於今年底見頂，但聯準會仍可能緩
步升息。市場預期，2026 年 12 月及 2027 年 3 月、4 月可能各升息一碼。歷史
經驗顯示，升息將推高實質利率、壓縮股市估值。經估算後，標普 500 指數預估
本益比不排除持續下修。儘管企業獲利成長，美股短期仍須留意估值修正風險 。  
◼ 展望 2027 年，美股企業獲利仍具成長動能，但在升息壓抑估值、獲利增速放緩
下，市場可能先震盪整理，待本益比與籌碼沉澱後再尋求上攻 。資產配置建議仍
以美股為核心，搭配台、韓股市作衛星配置，並關注高評級債券。

▎美國通膨未擴散，Fed 維持緩升格局 
聯準會主席 Warsh 傾向參考的通膨指標，PCE 細項年
增率與 6 個月年化增速大於 3%的比例，雖然高於疫情
前水準，但已較今年 4 月高點有顯著下滑(圖 3)。顯示
美國通膨雖尚未明顯降溫 ，但通膨壓力尚未全面擴散，
緩解二次通膨的疑慮，Fed 多位官員也早已明示將不急
於 10 月再次升息。展望未來，若以近 3 個月核心 CPI
月 增 率 均 值 0.24% 測 算 ， 核 心 CPI 年 增 率 可 望 在
2026/12 見頂，隨後逐步下滑(圖 4)。除非月增率持續
維持在 0.3%以上，否則時序進入 2027 年後，聯準會
將可隨著通膨下滑，逐漸調整貨幣政策鷹派力道。截至
10 月下旬， 期貨市場預期聯準會將在2026/12、2027/3、
2027/4 各升息 1 碼後，結束此波升息循環。

圖 3  美國物價壓力未全面擴散，尚無二次通膨擔憂

資料來源：Bloomberg, 2026/10/27

圖 4 核心 CPI 若維持近期趨勢，年增率年底可見頂

資料來源：Bloomberg, 2026/10/27 
不過，聯準會此波升息循環雖維持緩升步調，但對於金
融市場仍會帶來實質性緊縮的效果，參考 2000 年以來
的升息循環，升息期間與升息前，皆會帶動實質利率走
高，此反映資金成本的提升 ， 而在股債的資金排擠效應 ，
以及市場參與者在利率走升時 ，對於股票的估值將更為
嚴格下 ， 美股的預估本益比通常會在升息前與升息循環
期間遭到一定比例的壓縮，下行幅度則取決於升息的速
度。按照期貨市場預估 ，若此波升息循環持續到 2027/4，
即共升息４碼，平均每月升息幅度為 0.5 碼，與過去三
次升息循環比較 ， 升息節奏介於2004-2006 年與 2015-
2018 年升息循環。以升息前 3 個月到升息期間預估本
益比低點計算 ， 預 估 本 益 比 收 縮 幅 度 或 介 於
14.1%~20%。

對照此次升息前三個月到預估本益比低點，僅收縮約
13.4%，隨著升息效果逐漸傳導至實體經濟，金融環境
估持續收緊，若以 2004 年與 2015 年的升息循環做為
參考 ， 並以內插法計算 ， 此次升息循環標普 500 指數預
估本益比理論上最低或降至 17 倍(即升息前 3 個月到
最低點約收縮 17.6%)(圖 5)。 因此 ， 雖然美股2027 年、
2028 年 EPS 分別預估成長 19.5%、16.5%（圖 6），但
因預估本益比仍有下探空間 ， 加上明年企業獲利增速將
較今年顯著放緩，市場對於估值本就會更加保守，因此
需留意本益比的修正或使 2027 年美國股市，以及今年
以來累積漲幅驚人的半導體類股股價承壓。

▎美股待估值落底，歐股則需保守以對 
以全年角度而言，2027 年標普 500 指數企業獲利在主
要市場中 ， 僅落後給有AI 供應鏈題材的台灣加權指數 、
韓國 Kospi 指數 ，因此全年表現並不看淡， 只是短期美
國股市或陷入震盪 ， 待估值與籌碼沉澱後才有繼續上攻
空間。其他市場部分，歐洲股市展望仍偏向保守，因除
了短期有財政赤字擔憂，及 2027 年預算案卡關的政治
風險以外，因法國總統大選將於明年 4 月 18 日舉行第
一輪投票，若未有單一候選人取得絕對多數，由得票前
兩高的候選人於兩週後再進行第二輪投票 ， 因此最終結
果最晚於 5 月初才會出爐 。 參考市場隱含的勝選機率 ，
國民聯盟(RN)的黨魁 Le Pen 勝選機率最高(圖 7)， 其雖
然支持降低企業稅負，但風險在於 Le Pen 擬削減支出
以達成降低赤字目標 ， 其中削減對歐盟的相關支出為重
中之重，或使與歐盟關係出現鬆動，預期歐系資產在不
確定性消除前較難有所表現。

資產配置上，仍是以美股為核心配置，並搭配台、韓等
基本面佳的標的做為衛星配置 ， 此外 ， 考量Fed 可望於
明年下半年前結束升息循環，利率市場或提前反應，因
此也可關注高評級券種，做為穩定息收來源。 例如
PIMCO 收益增長基金，其投資標的涵蓋美股、新興市
場股票、美國固定收益等，且所持有的固定收益信用評
級以 AAA、AA 為主，除掌握全球股市多頭趨勢以外，
更可兼顧長期高評級券種的潛在資本利得空間。

圖 7 市場預期 Le Pen 將贏得 2027 年法國總統大選

摘要：
伊朗媒体称，美伊通过中间⼈交换信息属于常规做法，双⽅没有取得任何新进展，伊朗也不会
在美国落实其提出的七项条件前采取新的⾏动。
美股周⼀午盘⾛势分化，道指保持上涨，上述伊朗媒体消息后，标普 500 指数转跌约 0.1% ，纳
指跌幅扩⼤，纳斯达克 100 指数盘中⼀度跌近 0.9% 。国际原油则延续此前跌势，美油、布油盘
中跌近 8% 。
此后，美国总统特朗普表⽰，美国 “ 正在伊朗问题上取得胜利 ” ，并称： “ 油价今⽇下跌 10 美元，
为历史上最⼤单⽇跌幅。 ” 特朗普还向 Axios 表⽰，他 “ 不急于 ” 决定是否重启对伊朗的军事⾏动。
特朗普释放暂不急于恢复军事⾏动的信号后，纳斯达克 100 的跌幅迅速短线收窄到 0.8% 以内。

被问及是否考虑在美国中期选举之后再次打击伊朗时，特朗普回应：不会。此后标普⼀度微幅
转涨。
周⼀原油⼤跌主要反映市场对美伊外交进程的乐观预期：周末特朗普释放愿意给谈判更多时间
的信号，周⼀伊朗官⽅媒体⼜称，伊最⾼领袖已就和平谈判向政府授予 “ ⼀定权⼒ ” 。
伊朗称美伊沟通没有新进展，霍尔⽊兹海峡仍由伊⽅控制
据伊朗法尔斯通讯社（ Fars News ）报道，知情⼈⼠称，伊朗与美国通过中间⼈交换信息属于常
规做法，双⽅在这⼀渠道上没有出现任何新进展。伊⽅强调，在美国落实伊朗提出的七项条件
之前，不会有新的进展，也不会采取任何新⾏动。
该知情⼈⼠还声称，霍尔⽊兹海峡完全处于伊朗的军事控制之下，没有任何美国舰船敢于接近
海峡数百公⾥范围内。这⼀表态显⽰，尽管美伊仍通过第三⽅保持沟通，但双⽅在核⼼诉求和
海峡控制权问题上依然存在明显分歧。

判尚⽆突破。伊朗⽅⾯提出的七项条件涉及霍尔⽊兹海峡重新开放、美国解除对伊朗港⼝的海

尽管 Fars 有关沟通没有新进展的报道为外交前景增添不确定性，但该消息传出后，国际油价仍
维持接近 8% 的盘中跌幅，并未扭转此前的下⾏趋势。
美油盘中跌破 100 美元，布油跌⾄ 103 美元附近
国际油价周⼀⼤幅下挫，主要受到市场对美伊外交缓和的预期推动。周末特朗普与以⾊列总理
内塔尼亚胡通话时释放了愿意让谈判充分展开的信号，周⼀伊朗总统⼜称最⾼领袖已授权政府
推进和平谈判，令市场下调了美伊冲突短期内重新升级的⻛险溢价。

美股午盘时段刷新⽇内低点时，美国 WTI 原油期货跌⾄ 95.15 美元 / 桶，较上周五收盘下跌 8.97 美
元，⽇内跌幅超过 8.6% ；布伦特原油期货跌⾄ 103.28 美元 / 桶，较上周五收盘下跌 10.15 美元，
⽇内跌幅超过 8.9% 。此前，美油已在欧洲交易时段跌破 100 美元 / 桶关⼝。
不过，外交接触尚未转化为实质性协议，霍尔⽊兹海峡通航⻛险和军事冲突升级的可能性仍然
存在，地缘政治⻛险溢价能否持续回落仍有待观察。
以⾊列敦促美国选后⽴即恢复对伊⾏动，谈判前景仍存变数
不过，以⾊列⽅⾯显然希望美国采取更强硬的⾏动。
据央视新闻援引以⾊列媒体上周末的消息，特朗普与以⾊列总理内塔尼亚胡于 10 ⽉ 31 ⽇上周六
就美国与伊朗的谈判进⾏了⼀次 “ 直接⽽尖锐 ” 的通话。
消息⼈⼠称，内塔尼亚胡敦促特朗普在 11 ⽉ 3 ⽇美国中期选举结束后⽴即恢复对伊朗的军事⾏
动，并对⽬前在卡塔尔斡旋下进⾏的美伊谈判感到担忧。特朗普现阶段则希望让谈判充分展
开，不希望以⾊列⼲扰谈判进程。
